Visual reference: https://claude.ai/artifact/6zF3FgewiMA7pCZSk766Xt (v1; its PT trend, goal dial, streak, month calendar and week curve are REJECTED).\\r\\n\\r\\n## Situation\\r\\n- App: `projects/lsatu` (React + Vite + TS + Tailwind, TanStack Query present, Supabase via Lovable Cloud). Home = `/classroom` in the Classroom frame (`src/components/classroom/CONTEXT.md`).\\r\\n- Home code: `src/components/classroom/home/` - `useClassroomHome.ts`, `homeModel.ts` (pure, tested), `ClassroomHomeView.tsx`, `home.css`.\\r\\n- ~25 one-to-one students plus Joshua. Student copy follows `wiki/teaching-voice.md`; no em dashes.\\r\\n- Hard rules: completed student work never lost or modified (additive migrations only). Branch per session, PR to main. Lovable Cloud: Edge Functions deploy through Lovable, confirm with Joshua first. Frontend reaches lsatprep.study only via Lovable deploy. A teacher is never the student.\\r\\n- Done separately: PR #132 (sideways scrollbar).\\r\\n\\r\\n## Goal\\r\\nA sleek, high-end Home that (1) gets a student into practice in one click, (2) shows the performance insight that matters most for THIS student\'s recent work, and (3) puts every message and notification in one place.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Approach\\r\\n\\r\\n### 0. Feature-preservation checklist (applies to every phase)\\r\\nThe redesign must keep, until a named replacement ships:\\r\\n- Assigned work rows and their actions (`ClassroomHomeView.tsx:168`).\\r\\n- Timed-section link interception that shows the Section briefing (`ClassroomHomeView.tsx:197`, `SectionBriefing.tsx`).\\r\\n- Report-card seen tracking (`ClassroomHomeView.tsx:362`, `reportCardSeen.ts`).\\r\\n- Home message preview dismissal watermark.\\r\\n- Access gates in `HomeAccess` (messages, schedule, activities, drill): a hidden feature stays hidden.\\r\\n- Next seven days strip: markup, classes and behavior unchanged.\\r\\nExisting `ClassroomHomeView.test.tsx` cases stay green; new cases added per phase.\\r\\n\\r\\n### 1. Layout\\r\\nTop to bottom; two columns decided by the Home container\'s available width (CSS container query), not the viewport, so a docked side panel collapses it to one column.\\r\\n1. **Practice launcher** (the existing `QuestionFinder`, extended; one instance, one Ctrl/Cmd+K owner, lazy bank loading and focus trap kept) + inbox bell + greeting.\\r\\n2. **Three one-click actions**:\\r\\n   - Continue assigned work: the earliest-due eligible assigned row (not overdue-dismissed, not teacher-addressed); hidden when none. Timed sections still route through the briefing.\\r\\n   - Smart Drill: `kind: \'smart-drill\'` (adaptive, unchanged).\\r\\n   - Start a timed section: opens the launcher on its Section tab.\\r\\n3. **Performance area**: one main insight + up to three minor ones (fewer when evidence is thin).\\r\\n4. **Inbox panel** (right column): unified feed, Joshua\'s human-written messages pinned.\\r\\n5. **Next session** card (dark).\\r\\n6. **Next seven days**: unchanged.\\r\\n- Look: light and warm, dark cards for emphasis. Motion: count-up, settle-in, gentle pulse; off under `prefers-reduced-motion`.\\r\\n- Empty states: one line + one action.\\r\\n- Verification matrix: student account at narrow (390), mid (900), wide (1440), and side-panel-docked widths; keyboard-only pass; reduced motion; dark class (`html.dark`).\\r\\n\\r\\n### 2. Practice launcher (Joshua\'s \\"specific question / dropdown\\" idea)\\r\\nOne panel, three tabs, all through the existing launch codec (`src/lib/drillLaunch.ts`):\\r\\n- **Question**: PrepTest \\u001a Section \\u001a Question dropdowns plus free text (exists). Contextual action changes with depth: PT+Section selected \\u001a \\"Start section\\"; +Question \\u001a \\"Open question\\". LR/RC routing stays the finder\'s.\\r\\n- **Section**: PrepTest + Section, annotated with freshness (\\"24 of 25 unanswered\\", `sectionFreshness.ts:46`), fresh-only toggle, and the student\'s timer setting (standard / 52.5 / 70 / custom / untimed) carried into `kind: \'section\'` `timerMode`. RC freshness shown as unknown until RC history is connected.\\r\\n- **By type**: question-type multi-select + count + optional difficulty \\u001a `kind: \'filter\'` (`drillLaunch.ts:163`). Adaptive Smart Drill is never repurposed for this.\\r\\n- Shelves (search-matched in the same panel): Flagged (`flaggedService.ts`) and Needs review (journal review status). _Proposed to Joshua, not yet decided._\\r\\n\\r\\n### 3. Insight engine (fixed rules, no AI text)\\r\\nPure `pickInsights(signals, pin?)` in `src/components/classroom/home/insights.ts`, fixture-tested. Adapters feed it, one per source; the engine never reads Supabase.\\r\\n\\r\\nSource-to-lens matrix:\\r\\n| Lens | Source / reader | Coverage label |\\r\\n|---|---|---|\\r\\n| LR question types | NEW additive RPC `home_lr_type_accuracy(p_class_id, p_days int default 30)`: latest attempt per distinct question in window, excluding review/blind-review replays; returns qtype, questions, correct | \\"LR practice, last 30 days\\" |\\r\\n| Sections | `src/lib/sectionHistory.ts` reader (handles legacy score/time fallbacks) | \\"Timed sections\\" |\\r\\n| Section diagnosis | saved `analysis_json` via `sectionAnalysis.ts` categories (reading / fundamentals / process / test-taking); Home summarizes the top existing finding and links to its report | \\"From your last section\\" |\\r\\n| RC | `src/features/rc/api/rcProgress.ts` | \\"Reading Comprehension\\" |\\r\\n| Bootcamp | per-topic progress stores (e.g. `topics/*/lib/supabase-progress.ts`) | \\"Bootcamp: <name>\\" |\\r\\n| Assigned work | `useAssignedTodoFeed` status only: done / due / overdue counts. No scores inferred from copy | \\"Work Joshua assigned\\" |\\r\\n| Repeat mistakes | `wrong_answer_journal` by qtype; trap wording from `wajNotesService` notes where present | \\"Wrong Answer Journal\\" |\\r\\n| Pace / confidence | `attempts.time_ms`, `attempts.confidence` (LR only) | \\"LR practice\\" |\\r\\n\\r\\nSelection rules:\\r\\n- Each lens returns `null` (ineligible) or `{ score 0..1, evidence }`. Eligibility floors per lens (e.g. qtype: �8 distinct questions; sections: �1 completed in 21 days; RC: �2 passages in 21 days).\\r\\n- Score = 0.5 x recency (days since last activity in that source, decays over 14 days) + 0.5 x actionability (size of the gap the lens found, normalized per lens). Volume never compared across units.\\r\\n- Ties broken by a fixed lens order. Redundancy: at most one card per source; a section cannot feed both Sections and Section diagnosis unless the diagnosis names something the score card does not.\\r\\n- 0 eligible \\u001a a single \\"Start here\\" card (Smart Drill). 1-3 eligible \\u001a show only those.\\r\\n- Pinned lens becomes main if eligible; if ineligible, show it as main with its empty state + the engine\'s picks as minors, and flag it in Joshua\'s Hub as \\"pinned view has no data\\".\\r\\n- Every insight has: coverage label, sample size, date range, an **evidence drawer** (\\"See the questions behind this\\": exact addresses, from section `questions_json` or attempt rows), and actions. For a weak LR type: \\"Try new ones\\" (`kind: \'filter\'`, fresh only) and, when journal entries exist, \\"Review these\\" (journal). _\\"Review these\\" vs \\"Try new ones\\" pairing proposed to Joshua._\\r\\n\\r\\n### 4. Pinning\\r\\nAdditive table `home_insight_pins`: `class_id text primary key references profiles(class_id)`, `lens text not null check (lens in (<enum>))`, `params jsonb not null default \'{}\'`, `pinned_by uuid not null default auth.uid()`, `updated_at timestamptz default now()`.\\r\\nRLS: admin SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE (via existing admin role check); student SELECT where `class_id` = their own profile\'s class_id. Params validated per lens in the client and by a CHECK on known keys. Unpin = delete row. Hub control on the student\'s page. RLS tests: student cannot read another student\'s pin or write their own.\\r\\n\\r\\n### 5. Unified inbox\\r\\n- New, separately named hook `useHomeFeed()` (existing `useInbox` and its consumers untouched), built on TanStack Query keys shared by Home panel and bell so read state updates both.\\r\\n- Rows: message threads (one row per thread, latest message) + notification events. Badge unit = unread rows.\\r\\n- De-duplication: additive columns on `notifications`: `event_key text` (unique per user when present) and `origin text check in (\'human\',\'system\')`; on `messages` (or thread metadata) `origin` likewise. New writers set both. Legacy rows: a notification is hidden when a message in the same thread links to the same target within 5 minutes. Pinning uses `origin = \'human\'`; legacy messages matching the known automated templates in `classroomNotify.ts` / `sessionBundleNotify.ts` count as system.\\r\\n- Read: messages keep participant read timestamps; notifications keep `read` boolean. Mark-all-read scoped to the rows loaded and visible.\\r\\n- Notifications query: hidden types (`streak`, `signup`) filtered in SQL before pagination; unread count from a separate `count` query; page size 30 with \\"load more\\".\\r\\n- New event kinds (graded work, booking change) require extending the DB CHECK constraint on `notifications.type` (additive migration) and a named writer each:\\r\\n  | Event | Writer | Link |\\r\\n  |---|---|---|\\r\\n  | New assignment | `classroomNotify.ts` (exists) | assignment route |\\r\\n  | Notes / recaps | `sessionBundleNotify.ts` (exists) | recap route |\\r\\n  | Graded work | where grading completes (`activity_section_grades` flow) | report card |\\r\\n  | Session reminder | existing reminder writer, confirm | schedule |\\r\\n  | Booking change | booking Edge Function (Lovable deploy, confirm with Joshua) | schedule |\\r\\n  Writers are idempotent via `event_key`.\\r\\n\\r\\n### 6. Build order (each its own PR, each passes the preservation checklist)\\r\\n1. Layout + restyle, existing data only; launcher tabs Question + Section; actions; inbox panel showing today\'s message preview; Next seven days untouched.\\r\\n2. Insight engine: LR types (new RPC), Sections, Section diagnosis; evidence drawer.\\r\\n3. Unified feed + bell + dedup columns + notification query fixes.\\r\\n4. RC, bootcamp, assigned, mistakes, pace/confidence lenses; By-type launcher tab; pinning.\\r\\n5. New notification writers (graded, booking) - the booking one needs a Lovable deploy.\\r\\n6. Teacher view adopts the design.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Key decisions & tradeoffs\\r\\n- Rules, not AI text: predictable, never states a false thing.\\r\\n- Source adapters + coverage labels instead of one \\"all practice\\" number: honest, more code.\\r\\n- Separate `useHomeFeed` instead of rewriting `useInbox`: zero risk to current consumers, some duplication.\\r\\n- Dedup via additive `event_key`/`origin` + a legacy heuristic, instead of back-filling old rows: no data changes.\\r\\n- Container-query layout instead of viewport breakpoints.\\r\\n- Next seven days frozen.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Risks / open questions\\r\\n- Performance: Home adds RPC + section history + feed. Plan: parallel queries, skeletons, no waterfall; measure first contentful paint before/after on a student account.\\r\\n- Legacy dedup heuristic may miss or over-hide; accept for legacy only.\\r\\n- Exact decay and floor values need tuning on real student data (fixtures from 3 anonymized students).\\r\\n- Product items for Joshua: Flagged / Needs review shelves; \\"Review these\\" vs \\"Try new ones\\".\\r\\n\\r\\n## Out of scope\\r\\n- Public landing page. Goal score, streaks, latest PT, recent drills list, month calendar. AI insight text. Changes to how Smart Drill or assignments run.\\r\\n"}'}]

A star select is valid against\\r\\n  // both, returns the column once it exists, and simply omits it before then -\\r\\n  // which is exactly what the `?? null` below is for.\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\'section_history\')\\r\\n    .select(\'*\')\\r\\n    .eq(column, value)\\r\\n    .order(\'completed_at\', { ascending: false });\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (error) throw error;\\r\\n\\r\\n  return ((data ?? r.br_score : null,\\r\\n    brPercent: r.br_used ? r.br_percent : null,\\r\\n    brDelta: r.br_used ? r.br_delta : null,\\r\\n    brUsed: Boolean(r.br_used),\\r\\n    totalTimeMs: r.total_time_ms ?? 0,\\r\\n    avgTimeMs: r.avg_time_ms ?? 0,\\r\\n    unansweredCount: r.unanswered_count ?? 0,\\r\\n    byQtype: asTally(r.by_qtype_json),\\r\\n    byDifficulty: asTally(r.by_difficulty_json),\\r\\n    questions: asQuestions(r.questions_json),\\r\\n    analysis: (r.analysis_json as SectionAnalysis) ?? null,\\r\\n    completedAt: r.completed_at ?? r.created_at ?? new Date().toISOString(),\\r\\n  }));\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface SectionSummary {\\r\\n  sections: number;\\r\\n  /** Highest percent scored. null when nothing has been sat. */\\r\\n  bestPercent: number | null;\\r\\n  bestLabel: string | null;\\r\\n  averagePercent: number | null;\\r\\n  totalTimeMs: number;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Best and average across a set of sections, 7Sage\'s two headline tiles.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Averages the percent rather than the raw score, because sections are not all\\r\\n * the same length and a mean of raw scores across a 24 and a 26 is a number\\r\\n * that means nothing.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function summarizeSections(rows: SectionHistoryRow[]): SectionSummary {\\r\\n  if (rows.length === 0) {\\r\\n    return {\\r\\n      sections: 0,\\r\\n      bestPercent: null,\\r\\n      bestLabel: null,\\r\\n      averagePercent: null,\\r\\n      totalTimeMs: 0,\\r\\n    };\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  const best = rows.reduce((a, b) => (b.initialPercent > a.initialPercent ? b : a));\\r\\n  const sum = rows.reduce((n, r) => n + r.initialPercent, 0);\\r\\n\\r\\n  return {\\r\\n    sections: rows.length,\\r\\n    bestPercent: best.initialPercent,\\r\\n    bestLabel: sectionLabel(best),\\r\\n    averagePercent: Math.round(sum / rows.length),\\r\\n    totalTimeMs: rows.reduce((n, r) => n + r.totalTimeMs, 0),\\r\\n  };\\r\\n}\\r\\n      user_id: string;\\r\\n      display_name: string | null;\\r\\n      is_admin: boolean;\\r\\n      last_seen_at: string | null;\\r\\n    }>;\\r\\n    const nameMap = new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.user_id, r.display_name]));\\r\\n    const lastSeenMap = new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.user_id, r.last_seen_at]));\\r\\n    // RLS on user_roles blocks students from reading other users\' roles, so\\r\\n    // admin status is sourced from the RPC (which runs SECURITY DEFINER).\\r\\n    const adminSet = new Set(rows.filter((r) => r.is_admin).map((r) => r.user_id));\\r\\n\\r\\n    // Last message per conversation via DISTINCT ON RPC (get_latest_messages):\\r\\n    // exactly one row per conversation, computed in Postgres, no full-table\\r\\n    // download. RLS on `messages` still governs via SECURITY INVOKER.\\r\\n    const { data: msgs, error: msgsError } = await supabase\\r\\n      .rpc(\'get_latest_messages\', { conv_ids: convIds });\\r\\n    if (msgsError) {\\r\\n      console.error(\'[inbox] load messages failed:\', msgsError);\\r\\n      setError(\'Could not load your messages\');\\r\\n      setLoading(false);\\r\\n      return;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    const lastMsgMap = new Map<string, { body: string; created_at: string; sender_id: string }>();\\r\\n    for (const m of msgs ?? The old stopgap only inspected recently-fetched messages,\\r\\n    // so an attachment older than the cutoff lost its paperclip. Non-fatal: on\\r\\n    // failure the indicator is simply missing, so warn (don\'t blank the inbox).\\r\\n    const attachmentConvIds = new Set<string>();\\r\\n    const { data: attConvs, error: attsError } = await supabase\\r\\n      .rpc(\'get_conversations_with_attachments\', { conv_ids: convIds });\\r\\n    if (attsError) {\\r\\n      console.warn(\'[inbox] attachment presence read failed:\', attsError.message);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    for (const row of attConvs ?? []) {\\r\\n      attachmentConvIds.add(row.conversation_id);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    const enriched: Conversation[] = (convs ?? []).map((c) => {\\r\\n      const parts = (allParts ?? [])\\r\\n        .filter((p) => p.conversation_id === c.id)\\r\\n        .map((p) => ({\\r\\n          id: p.id,\\r\\n          conversation_id: p.conversation_id,\\r\\n          user_id: p.user_id,\\r\\n          last_read_at: p.last_read_at,\\r\\n          display_name: nameMap.get(p.user_id) ?? null,\\r\\n          is_admin: adminSet.has(p.user_id),\\r\\n          last_seen_at: lastSeenMap.get(p.user_id) ?? null,\\r\\n        }));\\r\\n      const lastRead = lastReadMap.get(c.id);\\r\\n      const lastMsg = lastMsgMap.get(c.id) ?? null;\\r\\n      const unread = !!(lastMsg && lastRead && new Date(lastMsg.created_at) > new Date(lastRead) && lastMsg.sender_id !== user.id);\\r\\n      return {\\r\\n        ...c,\\r\\n        participants: parts,\\r\\n        last_message: lastMsg,\\r\\n        unread,\\r\\n        home_cleared_at: homeClearedMap.get(c.id) ?? null,\\r\\n        hasAttachments: attachmentConvIds.has(c.id),\\r\\n      };\\r\\n    });\\r\\n\\r\\n    setError(null);\\r\\n    setConversations(enriched);\\r\\n    setLoading(false);\\r\\n  }, [user]);\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * wajNotesService - user-scoped reflection notes keyed by (class_id, qid).\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Matches the natural upsert key in wajService. ## assistant

The revision fixes most of the earlier issues. Two concrete gaps remain in the launcher: the existing codec has neither RC section routing nor a fresh-only filter. I also need to correct my earlier recommendation: the section-history reader does not fully handle legacy score/time fallbacks, so reusing it unchanged would still produce misleading values.

## assistant

## FLAWS

Most earlier issues are addressed. These material gaps remain:

1. **The launcher still promises capabilities the launch contract lacks.** `kind: 'section'` feeds the LR question bank; RC uses a separate route. `kind: 'filter'` has no fresh-only option, so “Try new ones” can repeat answered questions. See [Drill.tsx:540](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/pages/Drill.tsx:540), [drillRunSpec.ts:146](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/lib/drillRunSpec.ts:146), and [questionFinderSearch.ts:413](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/components/questionfinder/questionFinderSearch.ts:413).  
   **Fix:** Specify bank-aware section dispatch and an exposure-aware selection path, including insufficient-fresh-question behavior and preservation of the selected queue on resume.

2. **The proposed deduplication fields cannot reliably connect messages to notifications.** `event_key` exists only on notifications; `origin` distinguishes authorship but cannot identify matching events. Thread-level origin is also inadequate because one thread contains both human and automated messages. Notification uniqueness does not prevent duplicate chat inserts on retries. Existing writers perform separate inserts: [classroomNotify.ts:87](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/lib/classroomNotify.ts:87).  
   **Fix:** Store a shared event identity on each generated message and notification, make both writes retry-safe, and place origin on individual messages.

3. **Deduplication, unread counts, and read actions still disagree.** A separate notification count includes events hidden during client-side deduplication. Reading the visible thread does not necessarily clear its hidden notification. Furthermore, one “latest message” row can show a student reply or automated notice while burying Joshua’s unread human message; the current inbox checks only the latest message when calculating unread state. See [useInbox.ts:189](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/hooks/useInbox.ts:189).  
   **Fix:** Define one canonical visible-event model for counts and read actions, and explicitly select the human-message preview independently of the thread’s latest message.

4. **The new notification columns need protection in the existing write guard.** Students can update their notification rows to mark them read; the immutability trigger protects an enumerated list of columns. Merely adding `event_key` and `origin` leaves those new fields outside that protection. See [notification guard:120](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/supabase/migrations/20260506124235_9378ad65-24e0-49bb-8d59-0a8fc28211f2.sql:120).  
   **Fix:** Extend the database guard or restrict column update privileges, with tests proving students cannot alter event identity or origin.

5. **The section reader does not provide the claimed legacy fallbacks.** I overstated this in my previous review. It defaults missing `initial_total`, `initial_percent`, and `total_time_ms` to zero instead of deriving them from legacy fields; missing dates become the current time. It also does not restrict results to timed sections. Reusing it unchanged can manufacture zero scores, apparent recent activity, and a false “Timed sections” label. See [sectionHistory.ts:113](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/lib/sectionHistory.ts:113); untimed section analysis is explicitly supported in [sectionHistorySave.ts:237](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/lib/sectionHistorySave.ts:237).  
   **Fix:** Correct the adapter’s legacy normalization, preserve unknown dates, and use “Sections” unless timing is verifiable.

6. **The LR RPC cannot supply the engine’s promised evidence or recency.** It returns only type, question count, and correct count, while selection needs the latest activity date and the drawer needs exact supporting attempts. A separate unconstrained attempt query could disagree with the RPC’s latest-per-question exclusions. The universal question-address drawer also does not fit assignment-status or bootcamp-progress lenses.  
   **Fix:** Define source-specific evidence payloads; return recency from the RPC and provide an evidence query using exactly the same window and attempt-selection rules.

7. **Session reminders still have no identified writer or implementation phase.** “Existing reminder writer, confirm” does not establish that reminders are emitted. Phase five names graded work and booking changes only. A notification type and schedule link do not create a reminder.  
   **Fix:** Identify the existing producer or explicitly schedule its implementation, including delivery timing, cancellation/rescheduling handling, and idempotency.

## IDEAS

Only new additions, ranked by value/effort:

1. **Prioritize “Needs review” by the student’s actual review date. — High value / low effort**  
   The journal already stores `reattempt_due_at` and `next_action`. Within the accepted shelf, surface due reviews first and show the student’s own next step beside the question address. This makes the shelf a useful practice queue rather than another collection. Source: [wajNotesService.ts:31](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/lib/wajNotesService.ts:31).

2. **Offer “Practice this exact skill” from a section diagnosis. — High value / medium effort**  
   Some findings concern a feature or reasoning pattern within a question type. Section analysis already breaks results down by type, family, feature, reasoning, and difficulty; the launch codec supports question-feature filters. Where the identifiers match, prefill a focused practice selection and display its filters before launch. Sources: [sectionAnalysis.ts:160](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/lib/sectionAnalysis.ts:160), [drillLaunch.ts:163](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/lib/drillLaunch.ts:163).

VERDICT: REVISE

Visual reference: https://claude.ai/artifact/6zF3FgewiMA7pCZSk766Xt (v1; its PT trend, goal dial, streak, month calendar and week curve are REJECTED).\\r\\n\\r\\n## Situation\\r\\n- App: `projects/lsatu` (React + Vite + TS + Tailwind, TanStack Query present, Supabase via Lovable Cloud). Home = `/classroom` in the Classroom frame (`src/components/classroom/CONTEXT.md`).\\r\\n- Home code: `src/components/classroom/home/` - `useClassroomHome.ts`, `homeModel.ts` (pure, tested), `ClassroomHomeView.tsx`, `home.css`.\\r\\n- ~25 one-to-one students plus Joshua. Student copy follows `wiki/teaching-voice.md`; no em dashes.\\r\\n- Hard rules: completed student work never lost or modified (additive migrations only). Branch per session, PR to main. Lovable Cloud: Edge Functions deploy through Lovable, confirm with Joshua first. Frontend reaches lsatprep.study only via Lovable deploy. A teacher is never the student.\\r\\n- Done separately: PR #132 (sideways scrollbar).\\r\\n\\r\\n## Goal\\r\\nA sleek, high-end Home that (1) gets a student into practice in one click, (2) shows the performance insight that matters most for THIS student\'s recent work, and (3) puts every message and notification in one place.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Approach\\r\\n\\r\\n### 0. Feature-preservation checklist (applies to every phase)\\r\\nThe redesign must keep, until a named replacement ships:\\r\\n- Assigned work rows and their actions (`ClassroomHomeView.tsx:168`).\\r\\n- Timed-section link interception that shows the Section briefing (`ClassroomHomeView.tsx:197`, `SectionBriefing.tsx`).\\r\\n- Report-card seen tracking (`ClassroomHomeView.tsx:362`, `reportCardSeen.ts`).\\r\\n- Home message preview dismissal watermark.\\r\\n- Access gates in `HomeAccess` (messages, schedule, activities, drill): a hidden feature stays hidden.\\r\\n- Next seven days strip: markup, classes and behavior unchanged.\\r\\nExisting `ClassroomHomeView.test.tsx` cases stay green; new cases added per phase.\\r\\n\\r\\n### 1. Layout\\r\\nTop to bottom; two columns decided by the Home container\'s available width (CSS container query), not the viewport, so a docked side panel collapses it to one column.\\r\\n1. **Practice launcher** (the existing `QuestionFinder`, extended; one instance, one Ctrl/Cmd+K owner, lazy bank loading and focus trap kept) + inbox bell + greeting.\\r\\n2. **Three one-click actions**:\\r\\n   - Continue assigned work: the earliest-due eligible assigned row (not overdue-dismissed, not teacher-addressed); hidden when none. Timed sections still route through the briefing.\\r\\n   - Smart Drill: `kind: \'smart-drill\'` (adaptive, unchanged).\\r\\n   - Start a timed section: opens the launcher on its Section tab.\\r\\n3. **Performance area**: one main insight + up to three minor ones (fewer when evidence is thin).\\r\\n4. **Inbox panel** (right column): unified feed, Joshua\'s human-written messages pinned.\\r\\n5. **Next session** card (dark).\\r\\n6. **Next seven days**: unchanged.\\r\\n- Look: light and warm, dark cards for emphasis. Motion: count-up, settle-in, gentle pulse; off under `prefers-reduced-motion`.\\r\\n- Empty states: one line + one action.\\r\\n- Verification matrix: student account at narrow (390), mid (900), wide (1440), and side-panel-docked widths; keyboard-only pass; reduced motion; dark class (`html.dark`).\\r\\n\\r\\n### 2. Practice launcher (Joshua\'s \\"specific question / dropdown\\" idea)\\r\\nOne panel, three tabs, all through the existing launch codec (`src/lib/drillLaunch.ts`):\\r\\n- **Question**: PrepTest \\u001a Section \\u001a Question dropdowns plus free text (exists). Contextual action changes with depth: PT+Section selected \\u001a \\"Start section\\"; +Question \\u001a \\"Open question\\". LR/RC routing stays the finder\'s.\\r\\n- **Section**: bank-aware dispatch: an LR section launches `kind: \'section\'` (LR bank, `Drill.tsx:540`); an RC section launches through the RC route the finder already uses (`questionFinderSearch.ts:413`). PrepTest + Section, annotated with freshness (\\"24 of 25 unanswered\\", `sectionFreshness.ts:46`), fresh-only toggle, and the student\'s timer setting (standard / 52.5 / 70 / custom / untimed) carried into `kind: \'section\'` `timerMode`. RC freshness shown as unknown until RC history is connected.\\r\\n- **By type**: question-type multi-select + count + optional difficulty \\u001a `kind: \'filter\'` (`drillLaunch.ts:163`). Adaptive Smart Drill is never repurposed for this. \\"Fresh only\\" needs an exposure-aware path: add an optional `excludeAnswered?: boolean` to the filter body (additive, old links unaffected), resolved when the queue is built (`drillRunSpec.ts:146`) against the student\'s answered qids; the built queue is stored in the Run Record so resume replays the same questions. If fewer fresh questions exist than `count`, say \\"Only N new Flaw questions left\\" before start, with an option to include answered ones.\\r\\n- Shelves (search-matched in the same panel): Flagged (`flaggedService.ts`) and Needs review (journal review status). _Proposed to Joshua, not yet decided._\\r\\n\\r\\n### 3. Insight engine (fixed rules, no AI text)\\r\\nPure `pickInsights(signals, pin?)` in `src/components/classroom/home/insights.ts`, fixture-tested. Adapters feed it, one per source; the engine never reads Supabase.\\r\\n\\r\\nSource-to-lens matrix:\\r\\n| Lens | Source / reader | Coverage label |\\r\\n|---|---|---|\\r\\n| LR question types | NEW additive RPC `home_lr_type_accuracy(p_class_id, p_days int default 30)`: latest attempt per distinct question in window, excluding review/blind-review replays; returns qtype, questions, correct, last_attempt_at. Companion RPC `home_lr_type_evidence(p_class_id, p_qtype, p_days)` shares the SAME selection function, returning the qids behind the number | \\"LR practice, last 30 days\\" |\\r\\n| Sections | Home adapter over `section_history`, NOT the unchanged `sectionHistory.ts` reader (it zero-fills missing `initial_total`/`initial_percent`/`total_time_ms` and stamps missing dates as now, `sectionHistory.ts:113`). Adapter derives totals from `total_questions`/`initial_score`, leaves unknown dates unknown (excluded from recency), never infers timing | \\"Sections\\" (\\"Timed\\" only when `mode` proves it) |\\r\\n| Section diagnosis | saved `analysis_json` via `sectionAnalysis.ts` categories (reading / fundamentals / process / test-taking); Home summarizes the top existing finding and links to its report | \\"From your last section\\" |\\r\\n| RC | `src/features/rc/api/rcProgress.ts` | \\"Reading Comprehension\\" |\\r\\n| Bootcamp | per-topic progress stores (e.g. `topics/*/lib/supabase-progress.ts`) | \\"Bootcamp: <name>\\" |\\r\\n| Assigned work | `useAssignedTodoFeed` status only: done / due / overdue counts. No scores inferred from copy | \\"Work Joshua assigned\\" |\\r\\n| Repeat mistakes | `wrong_answer_journal` by qtype; trap wording from `wajNotesService` notes where present | \\"Wrong Answer Journal\\" |\\r\\n| Pace / confidence | `attempts.time_ms`, `attempts.confidence` (LR only) | \\"LR practice\\" |\\r\\n\\r\\nSelection rules:\\r\\n- Each lens returns `null` (ineligible) or `{ score 0..1, evidence }`. Eligibility floors per lens (e.g. qtype: �8 distinct questions; sections: �1 completed in 21 days; RC: �2 passages in 21 days).\\r\\n- Score = 0.5 x recency (days since last activity in that source, decays over 14 days) + 0.5 x actionability (size of the gap the lens found, normalized per lens). Volume never compared across units.\\r\\n- Ties broken by a fixed lens order. Redundancy: at most one card per source; a section cannot feed both Sections and Section diagnosis unless the diagnosis names something the score card does not.\\r\\n- 0 eligible \\u001a a single \\"Start here\\" card (Smart Drill). 1-3 eligible \\u001a show only those.\\r\\n- Pinned lens becomes main if eligible; if ineligible, show it as main with its empty state + the engine\'s picks as minors, and flag it in Joshua\'s Hub as \\"pinned view has no data\\".\\r\\n- Every insight has: coverage label, sample size, date range, an **evidence drawer**, and actions. Evidence is source-specific: question addresses for LR types (evidence RPC) and sections (`questions_json`); module list for bootcamp; the assignment rows for assigned work; passage list for RC.\\r\\n- From a section diagnosis, \\"Practice this exact skill\\": when the finding\'s type/feature ids match launch filters (`sectionAnalysis.ts:160` to `kind: \'filter\'` `qtypes`/`qfeatures`), prefill and show the filters before launch. For a weak LR type: \\"Try new ones\\" (`kind: \'filter\'`, fresh only) and, when journal entries exist, \\"Review these\\" (journal). _\\"Review these\\" vs \\"Try new ones\\" pairing proposed to Joshua._\\r\\n\\r\\n### 4. Pinning\\r\\nAdditive table `home_insight_pins`: `class_id text primary key references profiles(class_id)`, `lens text not null check (lens in (<enum>))`, `params jsonb not null default \'{}\'`, `pinned_by uuid not null default auth.uid()`, `updated_at timestamptz default now()`.\\r\\nRLS: admin SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE (via existing admin role check); student SELECT where `class_id` = their own profile\'s class_id. Params validated per lens in the client and by a CHECK on known keys. Unpin = delete row. Hub control on the student\'s page. RLS tests: student cannot read another student\'s pin or write their own.\\r\\n\\r\\n### 5. Unified inbox\\r\\n- New, separately named hook `useHomeFeed()` (existing `useInbox` untouched), on TanStack Query keys shared by Home panel and bell.\\r\\n- **Event identity**: additive nullable `event_key text` on BOTH `notifications` and individual `messages`, plus `origin text check in (\'human\',\'system\')` on individual messages (not threads). Generated writers (`classroomNotify.ts:87`, `sessionBundleNotify.ts:48`, new writers) compute one deterministic key per event (e.g. `assignment:<id>:assigned`) and insert both rows with it; partial unique indexes `(recipient, event_key) where event_key is not null` on each table make retries no-ops. Human-typed messages get `origin=\'human\'`, no key.\\r\\n- **Protection**: extend the notifications immutability trigger (`20260506124235_...sql:120`) to cover `event_key`/`origin`, and the same on messages; tests prove a student cannot change either.\\r\\n- **One canonical visible-event model**, computed server-side in one RPC `home_feed(p_limit, p_before)`: an event with a key appears once (message wins, notification suppressed); legacy rows without keys use the 5-minute same-link heuristic inside the same RPC. The badge count comes from the same RPC, so counts never include hidden rows.\\r\\n- **Read**: opening a visible event marks every row sharing its key read (message participant timestamp + notification boolean) via `home_feed_mark_read(event)`. Mark-all-read applies to visible events only.\\r\\n- **Thread rows**: preview is Joshua\'s latest unread human message when one exists, else the latest message; unread = any unread human message from Joshua in the thread, not only the latest (the latest-only check in `useInbox.ts:189` is not reused).\\r\\n- Hidden types (`streak`, `signup`) filtered in SQL before paging; page size 30 with load more.\\r\\n- New notification types need the DB CHECK constraint extended (additive). Writers:\\r\\n  | Event | Writer | Status |\\r\\n  |---|---|---|\\r\\n  | New assignment | `classroomNotify.ts` | exists, add key/origin |\\r\\n  | Notes / recaps | `sessionBundleNotify.ts` | exists, add key/origin |\\r\\n  | Graded work | grading completion (`activity_section_grades` flow) | new |\\r\\n  | Session reminder | none today (type defined, no producer, verified). New scheduled job: 24h and 1h before `start_at`; key `session:<booking_id>:<offset>`; skipped when cancelled or moved, re-keyed on reschedule | new; needs a scheduler (Edge Function cron via Lovable, confirm with Joshua) |\\r\\n  | Booking change | booking Edge Function | new, Lovable deploy |\\r\\n\\r\\n### 6. Build order (each its own PR, each passes the preservation checklist)\\r\\n1. Layout + restyle, existing data only; launcher tabs Question + Section; actions; inbox panel showing today\'s message preview; Next seven days untouched.\\r\\n2. Insight engine: LR types (new RPC), Sections, Section diagnosis; evidence drawer.\\r\\n3. Unified feed + bell + dedup columns + notification query fixes.\\r\\n4. RC, bootcamp, assigned, mistakes, pace/confidence lenses; By-type launcher tab; pinning.\\r\\n5. New notification writers: graded work; session reminders (scheduled job); booking changes. Reminders and booking need Lovable Edge Function deploys, confirmed with Joshua first.\\r\\n6. Teacher view adopts the design.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Key decisions & tradeoffs\\r\\n- Rules, not AI text: predictable, never states a false thing.\\r\\n- Source adapters + coverage labels instead of one \\"all practice\\" number: honest, more code.\\r\\n- Separate `useHomeFeed` instead of rewriting `useInbox`: zero risk to current consumers, some duplication.\\r\\n- Dedup via additive `event_key`/`origin` + a legacy heuristic, instead of back-filling old rows: no data changes.\\r\\n- Container-query layout instead of viewport breakpoints.\\r\\n- Next seven days frozen.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Risks / open questions\\r\\n- Performance: Home adds RPC + section history + feed. Plan: parallel queries, skeletons, no waterfall; measure first contentful paint before/after on a student account.\\r\\n- Legacy dedup heuristic may miss or over-hide; accept for legacy only.\\r\\n- Exact decay and floor values need tuning on real student data (fixtures from 3 anonymized students).\\r\\n- Product items for Joshua: Flagged / Needs review shelves (if yes, order Needs review by the student\'s own `reattempt_due_at` and show `next_action`, `wajNotesService.ts:31`); \\"Review these\\" vs \\"Try new ones\\"; whether session reminders are wanted at all.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Out of scope\\r\\n- Public landing page. Goal score, streaks, latest PT, recent drills list, month calendar. AI insight text. Changes to how Smart Drill or assignments run.\\r\\n"}'}]

## assistant

## FLAWS

Two material problems remain in the revised fixes. The other addressed findings can stay closed.

1. **The proposed RC dispatch opens one passage, not a section.** The finder’s RC link supplies `passage=<id>`. The player then creates a session with exactly that passage and `DEFAULT_CONFIG`, so this route does not preserve the selected full section or timer. This is confirmed by [RcPlayer.tsx:130](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/features/rc/pages/RcPlayer.tsx:130). The player separately supports multiple passage IDs and explicit configuration at [RcPlayer.tsx:141](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/features/rc/pages/RcPlayer.tsx:141).  
   **Fix:** Specify an RC section launch that resolves all passages in section order, creates a session with the intended timing configuration, and navigates using its session ID.

2. **Event-specific reads cannot safely use a conversation-wide read timestamp.** Suppose Joshua sends an unread human message, then an assignment event arrives. Opening the assignment and advancing `last_read_at` also marks the earlier human message read, even though the student only opened the assignment. That contradicts the revised promise to mark only the selected event’s rows read. The existing mechanism is explicitly conversation-wide: [useInbox.ts:247](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/hooks/useInbox.ts:247).  
   **Fix:** Keep generated-event read state separate from the conversation watermark; advance that watermark only when the conversation is read, and use event acknowledgements—including a fallback for message-only delivery—to synchronize generated duplicates.

## IDEAS

No additional ideas this round. The plan already contains enough useful additions; resolving these two behavioral contracts is more valuable than expanding scope.

VERDICT: REVISE
